# SO21 Worker Sign-In / Sign-Out Log

Updated: 2026-06-09 16:37 UK
Owner: Operations
Status: temporary utilisation tracker

## Purpose

This is a temporary staff clock-in board for SellerOne 2.1.

It exists so Luke and Operations can see whether workers are genuinely active, quiet, blocked, or finished.

This is not a replacement for task packets or proof files. It is a utilisation tracker.

## Rules

Operations must update this log on every 2-minute pass.

For every active worker or reviewer, record:

- lane
- role
- thread id
- job reference
- sign-in time
- last visible movement
- current state
- next action
- sign-out time when complete or replaced

## Current State Values

Use one of:

- working
- quiet_one_pass
- nudged
- quiet_two_passes
- blocked
- ready_for_review
- reviewing
- complete
- replaced

## Action Rules

If `quiet_one_pass`:

- nudge the worker with a focused prompt

If `quiet_two_passes`:

- mark blocked with exact reason, or
- replace with a fresh bounded worker if safe

If `complete`:

- route reviewer if proof needs review
- mark proved only when proof rules allow it
- refill the empty lane with the next safe packet
